> As for the oat flour, I did a little research and it seems that the warning not to consume it raw is likely due to the fact that raw oats can contain a toxin called phytic acid, which can inhibit the absorption of nutrients in the body. Additionally, raw oats may harbor bacteria or mold if not stored properly, which could cause digestive issues or illness. That being said, if you've been using oat flour in your shakes without any problems, it might be fine - but it's always better to err on the side of caution when it comes to our health. You could consider cooking the oat flour briefly or toasting it in the oven to neutralize any potential toxins or bacteria before adding it to your shakes.
